# Codex May Goals Progress

Working contract: `maygoals.md` is the source of truth. The full objective is intentionally impossible and decade-scale; this file tracks concrete progress without redefining completion downward.

## Current Status

- Overall goal: active, not complete.
- Current priority: Wave Family F alignment/motion scaffolding and Wave Family N accessibility proof while Wave Family M physical proof remains blocked by the unavailable/offline real iPhone.

- Previous capability addition through schema v14: `BracketerPhysicalDeviceProofChecklist` now renders Settings > About Device Proof at `settings.deviceProof.summary`, `settings.deviceProof.item.<proof-id>`, and `settings.deviceProof.matrix`, recording `0` physical proofs and the required real-iPhone evidence matrix instead of treating simulator evidence as device proof. `BracketerPhysicalCaptureMatrix` now renders the real-device lab plan at `settings.deviceProof.captureMatrix`, defining 8 physical capture scenarios and reporting `0 of 8 scenario proofs captured` until an actual iPhone run supplies evidence. `BracketerVerificationRunbook` now renders result-bundle and benchmark-command contracts at `settings.deviceProof.verificationRunbook` and `settings.deviceProof.benchmarkCommands`, documenting simulator/unit/UI/physical bundle paths without claiming physical proof. `BracketerPhysicalCaptureRunbookCatalog` now renders 8 per-scenario real-iPhone lab handoffs at `settings.deviceProof.captureRunbooks`, and `BracketerPhysicalResultBundleIndex` renders `0 of 8 scenario result bundles indexed` at `settings.deviceProof.resultBundleIndex` until recorded real-device proof entries exist. `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestor` now validates structured physical proof submissions, rejects missing or invalid attachment signatures before schema/runbook lookup or state mutation, rejects simulator destinations and missing artifacts/private markers, records result-bundle SHA-256, result-bundle-summary SHA-256, and typed result-bundle metrics evidence, replaces duplicate scenario entries, requires the hashed device identifier to be a SHA-256 hex digest once the primary bundle digests are valid, rejects retained `REPLACE_WITH_`, `REPLACE_AFTER_PHYSICAL_RUN`, and `<DEVICE-UDID>` template placeholders before semantic device-model checks, rejects generic or cross-scenario `.xcresult` filenames while allowing same-scenario rerun suffixes, rejects non-iPhone model identifiers unless they match `iPhoneN,M`, rejects iOS build labels unless they are dotted iOS versions or Apple build numbers, requires a manifest snapshot SHA-256 digest, a distinct result-bundle summary SHA-256 digest, and valid result-bundle metrics, rejects stale or future capturedAt timestamps, records accepted iOS build labels into the result-bundle index, rejects reviewer evidence that does not contain the selected runbook's evidence descriptors, rejects reviewer evidence that does not echo the submission's ISO-8601 capturedAt timestamp, rejects reviewer evidence that does not echo the exact result-bundle filename and SHA-256, rejects reviewer evidence that does not echo the result-bundle summary SHA-256, rejects reviewer evidence that does not include a passing result-bundle summary with `result=Passed` and `failedTests=0`, rejects reviewer evidence that does not echo metric-prefixed result-bundle counts/duration/attachment bytes, rejects reviewer evidence that does not echo the hashed device identifier, device model identifier, and iOS build label, and flips exactly the matching scenario only when the submission satisfies the runbook. `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestReadiness` schema v14 now renders the human-visible ingestor contract at `settings.deviceProof.proofIngestor`, reporting `0 of 8 physical submissions accepted`, the required submission fields (scenario id, valid attachment signature, physical destination, scenario-bound result-bundle filename, result-bundle SHA-256, result-bundle summary SHA-256, result-bundle metrics, manifest snapshot SHA-256, physical capturedAt timestamp, capturedAt timestamp in reviewer evidence, result-bundle filename in reviewer evidence, device identifier in reviewer evidence, device model identifier in reviewer evidence, iOS build label in reviewer evidence, iPhone model identifier, hashed device identifier, iOS version or build label, expected artifact ids, scenario-bound reviewer evidence), rejection rules (invalid attachment signature, simulator destination, result-bundle filename for a different scenario, missing artifacts/device labels, missing manifest snapshot SHA-256, missing result-bundle summary SHA-256, missing result-bundle metrics, invalid result-bundle metrics, stale capturedAt timestamp, future capturedAt timestamp, non-iPhone model identifier, missing hashed device identifier, invalid iOS build label, invalid SHA-256, result-bundle summary SHA-256 equals bundle SHA-256, reviewer evidence missing scenario descriptors, reviewer evidence missing capturedAt timestamp, reviewer evidence missing result-bundle filename, reviewer evidence missing result-bundle SHA-256, reviewer evidence missing result-bundle summary SHA-256, reviewer evidence missing result-bundle metrics, reviewer evidence missing passing result-bundle summary, reviewer evidence missing hashed device identifier, reviewer evidence missing device model identifier, reviewer evidence missing iOS build label, unreplaced template placeholder, Photos identifiers, raw image bytes, precise coordinates), and the - Previous capability addition through schema v15: `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestor` records signed result-bundle test-contract evidence, requires xcodebuild and xcresulttool version strings, requires a scenario-bound test identifier, rejects missing/invalid test contracts, rejects reviewer evidence that does not echo the compact test-contract tokens, and keeps the same delimiter-aware suffix-collision boundary used by metric evidence. `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestReadiness` schema v15 exposed `result-bundle test contract`, `xcodebuild version`, `xcresulttool version`, `scenario-bound test identifier`, `result-bundle test contract in reviewer evidence`, `missing result-bundle test contract`, `invalid result-bundle test contract`, and `reviewer evidence missing result-bundle test contract` without incrementing the `0 of 8` physical proof count.
- Previous capability addition through schema v16: `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestor` records signed result-bundle timing metadata with summary start/finish and scenario test start/finish times, requires `capturedAt` to fall inside the result-bundle test window, rejects missing/invalid timing metadata, rejects reviewer evidence that does not echo compact timing tokens, and preserves the same suffix-collision reviewer-evidence boundary. `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestReadiness` schema v16 exposes `result-bundle timing metadata`, `result-bundle summary start and finish time`, `scenario test start and finish time`, `capturedAt inside result-bundle test window`, `result-bundle timing metadata in reviewer evidence`, `missing result-bundle timing metadata`, `invalid result-bundle timing metadata`, `capturedAt outside result-bundle test window`, and `reviewer evidence missing result-bundle timing metadata` without incrementing the `0 of 8` physical proof count.
- Previous v17 capability addition: `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestor` records signed per-artifact attachment-manifest hashes keyed by every expected runbook artifact id, rejects missing, unexpected, or malformed attachment-manifest SHA-256 values, requires reviewer evidence to echo delimiter-bound `artifact.<id>.sha256=<digest>` tokens, preserves suffix-collision rejection, and carries the manifest into recorded proof accessibility/Codable output without storing Photos identifiers, image bytes, or coordinates. `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestReadiness` schema v17 exposes `per-artifact attachment manifest SHA-256 values`, `attachment manifest hashes in reviewer evidence`, `missing attachment manifest hashes`, `invalid attachment manifest hashes`, and `reviewer evidence missing attachment manifest hashes` without incrementing the `0 of 8` physical proof count.
- Previous v18 capability addition: `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestor` now binds each signed attachment manifest to the submitted result-bundle filename, scenario-bound result-bundle test identifier, and scenario test start/finish timestamps before artifact hashes can be trusted. Reviewer evidence must echo both the attachment-manifest result-bundle context and the per-artifact hash tokens, and suffix-collision evidence still rejects. `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestReadiness` schema v18 exposes `attachment manifest result-bundle filename`, `attachment manifest scenario test identifier`, `attachment manifest test start and finish time`, `attachment manifest result-bundle context in reviewer evidence`, `invalid attachment manifest context`, and `reviewer evidence missing attachment manifest context` without incrementing the `0 of 8` physical proof count.
- Previous v19 capability addition: `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestor` now reconciles signed `BracketerPhysicalResultBundleMetrics.durationMilliseconds` against the signed scenario test start/finish window before accepting a physical proof submission. Shorter or longer metric durations reject with `.resultBundleTimingDurationMismatch(...)`, exact 60-second fixture windows accept, and `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestReadiness` schema v19 exposes `result-bundle duration disagrees with test window` without incrementing the `0 of 8` physical proof count. This is a readiness/validation-schema bump only; `BracketerPhysicalProofSubmission.schemaVersion` did not change because the rule uses existing signed metrics and timing fields.
- Previous v20 capability addition: `BracketerPhysicalAttachmentManifest` now carries signed per-artifact byte counts for every expected runbook artifact, includes delimiter-bound `artifact.<id>.bytes=<count>` reviewer-evidence tokens, and exposes `attachment.totalBytes=<sum>` in its signed summary. `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestor` rejects empty, missing, unexpected, and nonpositive byte counts, rejects manifest totals that disagree with `BracketerPhysicalResultBundleMetrics.attachmentByteCount`, and rejects reviewer evidence that does not echo every byte-count token. `BracketerPhysicalProofSubmission.schemaVersion` is v2 because the signed attachment manifest gained a new field; `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestReadiness` schema v20 exposes the new required fields and rejection rules without incrementing the `0 of 8` physical proof count.
- Previous v21 capability addition: `BracketerPhysicalResultBundleSummary` now signs typed result-bundle summary status/title/expected-failure/skipped-test evidence, records it into accepted proofs, and replaces the loose passing-summary substring gate with delimiter-aware reviewer-evidence tokens. `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestor` rejects missing typed summaries, non-`Passed` statuses, blank titles, expected failures, skipped tests, and suffix-collision reviewer evidence. `BracketerPhysicalProofSubmission.schemaVersion` is v3 and `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestReadiness` schema v21 exposes the typed summary fields and rejection rules without incrementing the `0 of 8` physical proof count.
- Previous v22 capability addition: `BracketerPhysicalResultBundleSummary` now signs total, passed, and failed test counts beside status/title/expected-failure/skipped-test evidence. `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestor` requires positive total counts, nonnegative passed/failed counts, all-passed/no-failed summaries, exact summary-count reconciliation with `BracketerPhysicalResultBundleMetrics`, and delimiter-aware reviewer evidence for the expanded typed summary tokens. `BracketerPhysicalProofSubmission.schemaVersion` is v4 and `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestReadiness` schema v22 exposes the summary count fields and mismatch rejection rule without incrementing the `0 of 8` physical proof count.
- Previous v23 capability addition: `BracketerPhysicalResultBundleProofInput` now decodes `xcresulttool get test-results summary --compact` JSON into typed summary, metrics, timing, test-plan, device, and environment proof input, derives reviewer-evidence lines and a test-contract seed, rejects invalid attachment byte counts, invalid summary time windows, malformed JSON, and missing test-plan configuration, and `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestReadiness` schema v23 exposes the compact-summary parser fields/rejection rules without incrementing the `0 of 8` physical proof count.
- Previous v24 capability addition: `BracketerPhysicalResultBundleDevice` now records xcresult device model, physical platform, iOS version, and OS build metadata in signed physical proof submissions. `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestor` rejects missing device metadata, simulator xcresult platforms, non-iPhone device metadata, metadata that does not match the submitted iOS build label, and reviewer evidence that omits delimiter-bound result-bundle device tokens. `BracketerPhysicalProofSubmission.schemaVersion` is v5 and `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestReadiness` schema v24 exposes the device/platform contract without incrementing the `0 of 8` physical proof count.
- Previous v25 capability addition: parsed `BracketerPhysicalResultBundleProofInput` can now seed preview-only physical proof submission templates with compact-result summary, metrics, timing, device/platform metadata, reviewer-evidence lines, and attachment-manifest placeholders. `BracketerPhysicalProofSubmissionDocument` can prefill the same template from compact xcresult summary JSON when a caller supplies attachment byte count, and Settings/Shortcuts preview plumbing now routes parsed proof-input JSON named with a runbook id through the shared preview provider. Real hashes, manifest digest, physical device identifiers, per-artifact attachment hashes, and real reviewer evidence remain placeholders; v26 later hardened the per-artifact byte placeholders to zero while retaining the parsed attachment total only in metrics, so the physical proof count stays `0 of 8`. `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestReadiness` schema v25 exposed the parsed proof-input fields in Settings/UI.
- Previous v26 capability addition: `BracketerPhysicalResultBundleCommandPlan` now derives non-executing shasum, compact `xcresulttool`, `xcodebuild -version`, and `xcresulttool version` command lines from a scenario-bound `.xcresult` path, rejects unsafe or mismatched paths, exposes digest/summary/metrics/tool-version output paths, and reuses the existing proof-input parser without executing commands or incrementing physical proof counts. The same slice also hardened seeded proof-input templates so parsed attachment totals stay only in result-bundle metrics and all per-artifact byte counts remain zero placeholders until real lab evidence replaces them. `BracketerPhysicalProofIngestReadiness` schema v26 exposes the command-plan fields in Settings/UI.
- Previous v27 capability addition: `BracketerPhysicalResultBundleCommandPlanDocument` now turns the non-executing command plan into copy/share-only text, and Settings exposes it at `settings.deviceProof.proofIngestor.commandPlan` without running any process or incrementing physical proof counts.
- Previous v28 capability addition: `ExportBracketerPhysicalResultBundleCommandPlanIntent` now returns a Shortcuts `IntentFile` for a selected physical runbook scenario, backed by the same copy/share-only command-plan document used in Settings. The intent can omit the metrics extraction command, exposes an App Shortcut phrase, and keeps the no-execution/no-physical-proof boundary intact.
- Previous v29 capability addition: `ExportBracketerPhysicalProofTemplateIntent` now returns a Shortcuts JSON `IntentFile` seeded from compact xcresult summary JSON plus attachment byte count, using the same preview-only physical proof template path as Settings/import preview. The provider keeps real hashes, manifest digest, device identifiers, and reviewer-run evidence as placeholders while preserving parsed attachment totals only in result-bundle metrics and keeping per-artifact byte counts at zero.
- Previous v30 capability addition: `ExportBracketerPhysicalResultBundleCommandPlanIntent` now exposes a caller-provided `Result Bundle Path`, and `BracketerPhysicalResultBundleCommandPlanFileProvider` trims that path before feeding it into the existing scenario-bound, `.xcresult`, and shell-safety validation. Blank paths fall back to the runbook default, cross-scenario paths reject, and `BracketerPhysicalResultBundleCommandPlan.make` now trims direct custom input before deriving digest, summary, metrics, and tool-version artifact paths. This keeps Shortcuts command plans useful for rerun bundles outside the default lab directory without running commands or counting physical proof.
- Previous v31 capability addition: `BracketerPhysicalLabWorkspaceDocument`, `ExportBracketerPhysicalLabWorkspaceIntent`, and Settings `settings.deviceProof.proofIngestor.labWorkspace` now bundle a selected physical runbook, safe command plan, expected artifacts, output paths, and seeded proof-template JSON into a copy/share-only Markdown lab workspace. The workspace states `no physical proof captured`, keeps proof placeholders and zero per-artifact byte counts intact, rejects unsafe result-bundle paths through the existing command-plan validator, and still leaves physical proof at `0 of 8`.
- Previous v32 capability addition: lab workspaces now embed a structured `bracketer-physical-lab-workspace-manifest` JSON fence; `BracketerPhysicalLabWorkspaceDocument.decodeManifest`, `BracketerPhysicalLabWorkspaceReviewChecklist`, `BracketerPhysicalLabWorkspaceReviewPreviewProvider`, and `PreviewBracketerPhysicalLabWorkspaceIntent` decode that manifest into a review checklist without importing evidence or incrementing physical proof. The preview rejects missing manifests and runbook mismatches, checks current expected artifacts, requires the no-physical-proof boundary, requires output artifact paths including metrics only when the command plan includes metrics extraction, and reports `pure-model-proof`.
- Previous v33 capability addition: `BracketerPhysicalLabReviewHandoffPackage` now turns a lab workspace into one manifest-backed copy/share-only archive text with deterministic payload order, per-payload byte counts, SHA-256 digests, the workspace Markdown, command-plan text, seeded proof-template JSON, output-paths Markdown, and reviewer-checklist Markdown. `ExportBracketerPhysicalLabReviewHandoffPackageIntent` exports that archive as a single `IntentFile` without adding another App Shortcut, and Settings exposes it at `settings.deviceProof.proofIngestor.labReviewHandoffPackage`. The package keeps the workspace's `no physical proof captured` status, avoids Photos identifiers/raw bytes/precise coordinates, does not execute commands, and leaves physical proof at `0 of 8`.
- Previous v34 capability addition: Settings now exposes `settings.deviceProof.proofIngestor.labWorkspaceImportPreview`, a no-save row that opens plain-text or `.md` workspace files, decodes the embedded workspace manifest through `BracketerPhysicalLabWorkspaceReviewPreviewProvider`, and updates preview status without mutating runbooks, result-bundle indexes, or physical proof counts. The default row status explicitly says no workspace has been previewed and that proof counts remain unchanged.
- Previous v35 capability addition: `BracketerPhysicalLabReviewHandoffPackageReviewPreviewProvider`, `BracketerPhysicalLabReviewHandoffPackagePreviewFileProvider`, and `PreviewBracketerPhysicalLabReviewHandoffPackageIntent` now decode exported handoff-package archives back into preview checklists. The decoder validates the copy/share-only package boundary, unique archive filenames, per-block byte counts, SHA-256 digests, manifest JSON readability, manifest schema version, required payload inventory/kinds, manifest-to-block metadata, and embedded workspace preview before reporting `pure-model-proof`, and it still does not mutate runbooks, result-bundle indexes, or physical proof counts.
- Current v36 capability addition: Settings now exposes `settings.deviceProof.proofIngestor.labReviewHandoffPackageImportPreview`, a no-save row that opens plain-text or `.txt` handoff-package archives, decodes them through `BracketerPhysicalLabReviewHandoffPackageReviewPreviewProvider`, surfaces package/workspace checklist status, and keeps runbooks, result-bundle indexes, and physical proof counts unchanged. The focused UI contract now requires the default row text to say no handoff package has been previewed, `physical-lab-review-handoff preview only`, `Import preview does not mutate runbooks or result-bundle indexes`, and `No physical proof count changed`.
- Current v37 capability addition: the existing `BracketerPhysicalDeviceLabPreflight` now exposes a stable `checkIDs` projection so tests and future lab handoff exporters can assert the reviewer checklist ids without remapping `checks.map(\\.id)` at each call site. The existing `settings.deviceProof.deviceLabPreflight` row remains the only visible preflight surface; a duplicate attempted row was removed after Claude and `xcodebuild` caught the invalid redeclaration risk.
- Current v38 capability addition: `AdaptiveCapturePlanningProfile` now gives the deterministic bracket recipe planner a typed adaptive-capture profile with intent, scene condition, dynamic range, motion/stability, highlight/shadow risk, lens capability status, source signals, recommended plan, confidence, explanation, and explicit no-raw-photo/no-Photos-ID/no-coordinate/no-physical-proof privacy boundary. `DeterministicBracketRecipePlanner` now consumes that profile while preserving the existing public recommendation titles, plans, and confidence values.
- Current v39 capability addition: `BracketerHostDeviceAvailabilityReport` now parses reviewer-supplied `devicectl list devices` and `xctrace list devices` text into privacy-safe physical-iPhone availability counts, redacting raw device identifiers into deterministic hashed row ids and ignoring simulator rows. It also derives a lab-readiness verdict for blocked/no-device/available-but-still-not-proof host reports. Settings exposes the no-save import-preview row at `settings.deviceProof.deviceAvailabilityReportImportPreview` with default text that says no report has been previewed, `host-device-availability preview only`, `Connected unlocked iPhone still required`, and `No physical proof count changed`.
- Current v40 capability addition: `AdaptiveCapturePlanningProfile` now includes a typed `CaptureStrategy` with timer, format, lens, and stabilization recommendations. The strategy is derived only from typed scene text, `CaptureContextSummary`, and device capability summaries; lens recommendations are capability-summary guidance, not active-lens proof. Deterministic bracket recipe actions now draw from those strategy facts while preserving the existing recommendation shape and validator boundary.
- Current v41 capability addition: host availability report readiness verdicts are now covered for unavailable/offline/no-device paths and for an available physical iPhone path that still requires signed physical lab artifacts before any proof count can change.
- Current v42 capability addition: the focused Apple Intelligence fallback UI path now proves the bracket recipe recommendation row, source evidence row, Apply action, applied recipe summary, recent recipe row, active bracket strip, and review manifest recipe from the same deterministic fallback recipe without flattening evidence accessibility or hiding the Apply button. The first rerun caught the recommendation identifier/evidence exposure regression; the final shell `xcodebuild` run passed.
- Current v43 capability addition: `BracketRecipeRecommendation.compactEvidenceSummary` now gives Settings > AI a compact confidence/source-signal evidence row at `settings.intelligence.recipe.evidence.<index>`, and deterministic planning treats high-contrast/bright-window language as wide dynamic range without escalating it to extreme HDR unless explicit HDR/extreme wording or severe clipping exists.
- Current v44 capability addition: `BracketerAccessibilityAudit` now gives Settings > About a model-backed inclusive-design audit at `settings.accessibility.audit`, with rows for Dynamic Type, Reduced Motion, High Contrast, and Tap Targets. - Current v45 capability addition: `BracketerReducedMotionContract` backs the Reduced Motion audit row, and the main camera/settings shell now reads `accessibilityReduceMotion` to collapse spring/move transitions to opacity or no animation for camera chrome toggles, Settings sheet presentation/dismissal, toast transitions, app-intent panel routing, and the Settings grid preview while preserving capture timing and haptics. The audit also records observed SwiftUI/UIAccessibility environment evidence at `settings.accessibility.environment`, with a deterministic forced-accessibility UI-test mode for Dynamic Type, Reduce Motion, and High Contrast observation without claiming physical-device proof.
- Current v46 capability addition: `BracketerHighContrastContract` backs the High Contrast audit row and verifies the first scoped Settings > About pattern: status is never color-only, every row keeps icon/text/accessibility-value status, and Increased Contrast strengthens audit row borders. Unit coverage proves default final-output blockers expose concrete plan names/reasons, injects a custom one-plan/two-blocker manifest to prove blocker detail is detail-only and does not fail the workspace gate by itself, and preserves missing-plan/unexpected-final-byte failure proof.

### Previous slice: final-workspace archive-integrity injection coverage v1

- Implementation evidence:
  - Claude was invoked with `claude --dangerously-skip-permissions` for a read-only v105 inspection pass and flagged the main design risk: constructing `BracketProjectExportBundle` in the review fixture default path would do heavy base64/PDF/JPEG work during UI render.

### Previous slice: project-review before/after scrub plan v1

- Implementation evidence:
  - `BracketProjectBeforeAfterScrubPlan` derives a deterministic before/after scrub plan from existing manifest exposure comparison and side-by-side synthetic fixture pixels.
  - When the selected shot is the baseline, the model chooses the nearest guard exposure; when the selected shot is a guard, it compares that selected frame against the baseline.
  - `BracketProjectReviewHandoffView` renders the card at `review.project.beforeAfterScrub.card` with baseline/compare pills, comparison role, five scrub-stop swatches, and a no-Photos/no-final-HDR boundary.
  - `BracketProjectReviewAccessibilityContract` now requires `review.project.beforeAfterScrub.card`, so the selected-project review workspace contract cannot silently drop the scrub surface.
  - `BracketerAccessibilityScreenshotMatrix` now lists `review.project.beforeAfterScrub.card` as a required Project Review Handoff identifier for future accessibility screenshot proof.
- Unit test evidence:

## Parsed Checklist

Legend:

- `[ ]` not started or insufficient evidence.
- `[~]` partially implemented or current slice underway.
- `[x]` verified complete for the stated acceptance criteria.
- `[blocked]` blocked with evidence.

### Global Rules And Evidence Discipline

- [~] Inspect live repo before editing.
  - Acceptance: current source, docs, tests, CI, verification commands, and project instructions inspected and recorded here.
  - Evidence: repo inspection commands listed above.
- [ ] Preserve unrelated user changes.
  - Acceptance: no unrelated reverts; final diff reviewed.
- [ ] Keep implementation iPhone-native, SwiftUI-forward, AVFoundation-truthful, Photos-correct, and privacy-preserving.
  - Acceptance: new code uses repo-native Swift/Apple frameworks, no cloud services, no fake camera claims.
- [ ] Keep proof categories separate.
  - Acceptance: each verification entry is tagged as pure-model-proof, simulator-ui-proof, local-sdk-proof, runtime-diagnostic-proof, physical-device-proof, manual-review-proof, or blocked-proof.
- [ ] Major features have model, behavior, tests, docs or ledger, and verification.
  - Acceptance: every completed wave lists changed files, tests, docs, and command result evidence.

### Impossible Completion Criteria

- [ ] Live-intent bracket planning from scene data, device capability, lens, motion, histogram, clipping risk, dynamic range, format, and export goal.
  - Acceptance: production planner plus deterministic tests and UI/runtime proof.
- [~] Closed capture loop verifies bracket sufficiency and recovery captures.
  - Acceptance: quality model, recovery recommendations, UI/runtime proof, tests.
  - Current evidence: `BracketProjectCaptureQualityReport` derives manifest-backed available/missing/failed counts, EV spread, highlight/shadow guard counts, RAW/processed representation counts, readiness score, findings, and recovery recommendations; export/import tests and restored-project UI tests passed. This is not a true closed loop yet because it does not inspect sharpness, alignment, ghosting, physical Photos resources, or automatically stage recovery captures.
- [ ] RAW/processed ingest, pairing, metadata preservation, missing-resource detection, and partial Photos-save recovery.
  - Acceptance: pair model, resource summaries, tests, physical-device checklist/proof.
- [ ] Local HDR reconstruction from bracketed captures.
  - Acceptance: truthful pipeline, fixture tests, performance diagnostics, docs.
- [ ] Alignment, ghosting, subject movement, and explainable deghosting strategy.
  - Acceptance: alignment/deghost models, synthetic tests, review UI evidence.

### Wave Family A: Project Spine And Persistence

- [x] Turn latest-capture state into persistent bracket projects.
  - Acceptance: `BracketProject` model owns manifest/review summaries/sidecars/project lifecycle and can be persisted.
- [~] Make every capture recoverable after app relaunch.
  - Acceptance: store survives re-instantiation or app relaunch in deterministic test.
  - Evidence: `fileBracketProjectStorePersistsCurrentLatestAndDeletionAcrossInstances` proves persistence across store instances; `testSimulatedBracketCaptureCompletesAndOpensReview` now terminates/relaunches the app and verifies `camera.project.latest` plus Settings project-library recovery for the saved simulated project. Photos-backed physical relaunch proof remains incomplete.
- [x] Make simulated and Photos-backed projects share one model.
  - Acceptance: factory paths for simulated and Photos manifests use same `BracketProject`.
  - Evidence: `CameraController.recordLatestBracketProject(...)` is called from simulated and Photos-backed manifest completion paths.
- [x] Keep raw image bytes out of metadata stores unless explicitly required.
  - Acceptance: model privacy flags prove no raw bytes are stored.
  - Evidence: `BracketProject.PrivacySnapshot.storesRawPhotoBytes == false` covered by unit tests.
- [x] Keep Photos identifiers and location information privacy-scoped.
  - Acceptance: privacy policy model distinguishes asset identifiers and coordinates.
  - Evidence: privacy snapshot distinguishes Photos identifier storage from raw bytes and precise coordinates; sidecar omission policy is documented and tested.
- [x] `BracketProject` or `ImagingProject` pure model.
  - Acceptance: Codable/Equatable/Sendable model with stable ID and lifecycle.
  - Evidence: `BracketProject` in `Bracketer/BracketProject.swift`; unit JSON round-trip passed.
- [x] Project store abstraction.
  - Acceptance: protocol or concrete store with save/load/list/delete/current/latest.
  - Evidence: `FileBracketProjectStore` exposes save/load/loadAll/current/latest/setCurrent/delete.
- [x] Codable persistence format or local database.
  - Acceptance: round-trip tests and corrupt data handling.
  - Evidence: JSON project files plus index; tests cover persistence, store re-instantiation, and corrupt project data.

## Blockers

- Physical-device proof cannot be produced until the connected `Physical iPhone` is unlocked for Xcode. The destination and signing path are visible, but run-destination preflight blocked with `"Unlock Physical iPhone to Continue"` and the incomplete `/tmp/bracketer-physical-readiness-device-tests-1779959100.xcresult` bundle cannot be read.
- Do not claim physical iPhone proof from simulator work, preview files, template files, or locked-device preflight.

## Next Action

Next pure-model/simulator slice: deepen Wave Family F/G/N into richer synthetic feature-matching edge cases, review/export ergonomics follow-up scenarios, final-output handoff scaffolding, or accessibility screenshot coverage, with focused model/UI assertions and no unsupported physical-proof claims. Physical-device slice remains blocked until the connected iPhone is unlocked; after unlock, rerun the focused physical runtime proof and promote exactly one physical capture matrix scenario from plan to evidence with a readable `.xcresult`, hashed result-bundle digest, manifest snapshot digest, fresh capturedAt timestamp echoed in scenario-bound reviewer evidence, and no private Photos identifiers, raw image bytes, decoded RAW bytes, thumbnail pixels, final rendered output bytes, or precise coordinates.
